       1| # AmiClaw 戰略路線圖：演進比較與開發優先事項
       2| 
       3| 本文件比較了 **AmiClaw** 與 **claude-code** 的演進軌跡，並概述了 AmiClaw 為了彌補功能差距並在代理編排 (agent orchestration) 領域建立市場領導地位所需的必要步驟。
       4| 
       5| ## 1. 功能比較 (Feature Comparison)
       6| 
       7| | 功能類別 | AmiClaw (編排器/Orchestrator) | claude-code (介面/Interface) |
       8| | :--- | :--- | :--- |
       9| | **架構 (Architecture)** | **以提供者為中心且模組化 (Provider-Centric & Modular)**：高度專注於 "Provider Pattern" (提供者模式，如 FileSystem, Atlassian 等) 以及協調者/工作者 (Coordinator/Worker) 代理模型。 | **以協定為中心且整合化 (Protocol-進Centric & Integrated)**：專注於 "Agent Communication Protocol (ACP)" (代理通訊協定) 與標準化的工具調用生命週期。 |
      10| | **能力 (Capabilities)** | **領域特定擴展 (Domain-Specific Expansion)**：強調專用代理 (如 UEFI, WhatsApp) 與基礎設施自動化 (Docker/Sandbox)。 | **豐富的開發者體驗 (Rich Developer Experience, DX)**：專注於 "Artifacts" (產出物，如 Mermaid, 語法高亮) 以及進階的終端/Shell 整合。 |
      11| | **上下文管理 (Context Management)** | **基於檢索 (RAG) 的重點**：使用 TF-IDF 延遲工具與 `HistoryComproll` 來管理長期記憶與溢出。 | **效率與邊界重點**：使用 `/compact` 演進與對 `getSessionMessages` 的嚴格邊界限制，以維持 Token 穩定性。 |
      12| 
      13| ## 2. 功能差異 (Functional Differences)
      14| 
      15| ### AmiClaw: 「生態系統構建者」(The "Ecosystem Builder")
      16| AmiClaw 的發展歷程展現了對 **擴展性與垂直整合** 的專注。其演進是由能夠接入新的 "Providers" 與 "Specialized Agents" 的能力所驅動。其設計旨在成為複雜、多領域工作流 (例如從 WhatsApp 到 Firmware 審查) 的骨幹。
      17| 
      18| ### claude-code: 「高保真度介面」(The "High-Fidelity Interface")
      19| claude-code 的發展歷程展現了對 **互動品質與環境穩健性** 的專注。其演進是由 "Artifacts" 革命 (程式碼/圖表視覺化) 以及讓代理感覺像是開發者終端 (Windows/PowerShell/Git Bash 優化) 的原生化驅動。
      20| 
      21| ## 3. AmiClaw 優先事項清單 (戰略待辦事項)
      22| 
      23| 為了達到或超越 `claude-code` 的功能，AmiClaw 必須從「強大的引擎」演進為「高保真度的開發者體驗」。
      24| 
      25| ### 第一階段：視覺與互動豐富度 (解決 "Artifacts" 差距)
      26| - [ ] **實作 AmiClaw Artifacts Engine**：開發一個 Webview 用戶介面渲染器，能夠顯示 Markdown、Mermaid 圖表與語法高亮的程式碼區塊。
      27| - [ ] **增強型 Markdown 上傳**：支援透過 Providers 上傳的文檔資產的直接渲染。
      28| 
      29| ### 第二階段：協定標準化與安全性 (解決 "可靠性" 差距)
      30| - [ ] **Ami-ACP (Agent Communication Protocol)**：為 Coordinator 與 Worker 的互動建立版本化的通訊標準，以防止在複雜工作流中的破壞性變更。
      31| - [ ] **工作流 Schema 驗證**：為 Workflow Engine 實作嚴格的 JSON/YAML schema 驗證，以確保 `AutoPilot` 序列在執行前結構正確。
      32| - [ ] **標準化工具調用生命週期**：實作一套統一的方法，用以追蹤、記錄並重試所有 Providers 中的工具調用失敗。
      33| 
      34| ### 第三階段：優化與環境穩健性 (解決 "效能" 差距)
      35| - [ ] **提示詞快取與路由優化**：為支援的 LLM 提供者實作「黏性」提示詞快取鍵 (sticky prompt cache keys)，以降低延遲與成本 (模擬 OpenAI 整合)。
      36| - [ ] **跨平台 Shell 抽象化**：增強 `FileSystem` 與 `DevTools` providers，包含 Windows (Powerstream) 與 Git Bash 環境的「自動相容模式」。
      37| - [ ] **會話邊界管理**：對訊息快取限制實施更嚴格的邊界，以防止長期間會話中因上下文導致的效能下降。
      38| 
      39| ### 第四階段：可觀察性與智能化 (解決 "進階" 差距)
      40| - [ ] **進階使用量分析**：擴展現有的使用量統計面板，包含每個 Provider 的 Token 成本追蹤。
      41| - [ ] **影子模式擴展 (Shadow Mode Expansion)**：擴展 "Shadow Mode" 功能，允許在不影響即時編排引擎的情況下測試新的 Provider 邏輯。

      94| ## 三、重構優先順序
      95| 
      96| ### P0 — 本次已完成 ✅
      97| 
      98| | 項目 | 影響 |
      99| |---|---|
     100| | Context 溢出：ollamaGetContextLength 優先讀 num_ctx | 每次 session 溢出 |
     101| | keepTail/dropFallback 8000 chars 截斷防溢出 | 長 session 崩潰 |
     102| | AutoPilot recordAutoPilotDenial/Success 接線 | 斷路器失效 |
     103| | _agentStatusRunning 解耦 streamEnd | Coordinator 按鈕錯亂 |
     104| | breathLight HTML 補回 + CSS breatheIdle/breatheActive 雙狀態動畫 | 視覺回饋消失；心跳永不停止 |
     105| | replace_in_file fuzzy hint + 多匹配偵測 | LLM 編輯失敗率高 |
     106| | _checkDangerousCommand 6 種安全阻擋 | 安全漏洞 |
     107| | Tool 描述重寫（claude-code 指令式風格） | 模型使用工具錯誤率 |
     108| | **handleAskWithTools isAgentMode 參數**：Ask 模式 system prompt 及唯讀工具篩選動態化 | Agent 模式被誤加唯讀限制 |
     109| | **updateModelSelect race condition 修復**：prevUiValue 優先保留使用者選擇 | 模型頻繁被重置 |
     110| | **Shadow Supervisor clearTimeout 修復**：Promise.race 後正確清除計時器 | 300s 後誤報超時 log |
     111| | **Shadow Supervisor retry**：null 結果等待 100s 重試最多 3 次 | 影子督促單次失敗即放棄 |
     112| | **Memory 系統修復**：_ltmCache eager-load、findRelevantMemories 分數公式修正（舊→新）、MEMORY.md 雙重注入防止 | 記憶遺忘 / 注入空內容 |
     113| | **HeartbeatService 升級**：AgentHeartbeatInfo 狀態追蹤、AgentExecutor 連動、dispose() 不停止 interval | 心跳停頓 / 無狀態廣播 |
     114| | **WA 定期回報**：notifyOwner + _lastActiveSendJid + 每 60s 推送 Agent 進度 | WhatsApp 無法感知 Agent 狀態 |
     115| | **Webview ready race condition 修復**：Promise-based _readyPromise + _messageQueue flush | 訊息在 webviewReady 前丟失 |
     116| | **isChoiceConfirmation**：偵測「選項 A/B 請確認」類型並自動授權繼續 | Agent 卡在等待使用者確認 |
     117| | **JSON tool-call error retry**：偵測 invalid tool call JSON 錯誤等待 100s 重試最多 5 次 | Ollama JSON 格式錯誤導致 Agent 終止 |
     118| | **Ask mode 路由 debug log**：send 訊息夾帶 _dbgModeSelect/_dbgAgentMode | 無法診斷 Ask/Agent 切換原因 |
     119|

     157| ## 四、AmiClaw 獨有優勢
     158| 
     159| | 功能 | 說明 |
     160| |---|---|
     161| | Shadow Staging Panel | VS Code diff toolbar，Sandbox commit/rollback |
     162| | 多模型並排比較 | Compare mode：A/B/C 同問題並排 |
     163| | Team Discussion modes | 5 種協作模式 |
     164| | Debate Engine | 兩模型辯論 + 裁判 |
     165| | WhatsApp 整合 | Agent 可透過 WA 控制 |
     166| | Jira/Bitbucket/Jenkins 工具 | 完整 DevOps 整合 |
     167| | UEFI Code Review Skill | AMI 固件審查知識注入 |
     168| | Compact 視覺化 bar | Before→After token 進度條 |
     169| | Thinking 燈動畫 | 軌道動畫 + 4 種等級 |
     170| 
     171| ---
     172| 
     173| ## 五、技術債摘要
     174| 
     175| ### 5.1 ollama-chat.ts 仍是 God Class
     176| ```
     177| 建議拆分 →
     178|   OllamaChatPanel.ts (UI only)
     179|   ChatSessionManager.ts (session state)
     180|   AgentRuntimeCoordinator.ts (routing + wiring)
     181|   MemoryCoordinator.ts (LTM + session notes)
     182| ```
     183| 
     184| ### 5.2 ToolExecutor giant switch（1800+ 行）
     185| ```typescript
     186| // 目標：每個工具實作 ToolHandler 介面
     187| interface ToolHandler {
     188|   validate(args): string | null;
     189|   classify(): ToolEffect;
     190|   execute(args, ctx): Promise<string>;
     191|   summarize(result): string;
     192|   audit(args, result): AuditEntry;
     193| }
     194| ```
     195| 
     196| ### 5.3 TF-IDF 精準度
     197| ```typescript
     198| // 需升級為真正的餘弦相似度
     199| const idf = buildIDF(EXTRA_TOOLS);  // 在 extension 啟動時建立索引
     200| function searchTools(query: string): Tool[] {
     201|   const queryVec = tfidf(query, idf);
     202|   return cosineRank(EXTRA_TOOLS, queryVec).slice(0, topK);
     203| }
     204| ```

FILE: _audit_3.md
LINE COUNT: 12

       1| # Chat message rendering — audit note
       2| 
       3| Chat messages are rendered **inline HTML** inside a VS Code webview; no external rendering library is used.
       4| - `src/webview/WebviewRenderer.ts` — single file generating the entire webview HTML/CSS/JS via `getHtmlForWebview()` (extracted from `OllamaChatPanel.getHtmlForWebview`).
       5| - Messages are appended to `#chat` as `.msg`/`.bubble` divs; DOM is built with `innerHTML` string templates + `createElement`.
       6| - Markdown is handled by a **custom lightweight renderer**, not a library:
       7|   - `parseBlocks()` splits text vs. ``` code fences; `renderTextBlock()` handles headings, lists, task items, tables, `$$` math blocks; `renderInline()` does inline formatting.
       8|   - `highlightCode()` is an embedded tokenizer (JS/TS/Python/Shell/CSS/JSON) with `hl-*` CSS classes; other languages are escaped plaintext.
       9|   - `rerenderBubbleMd()` re-renders a bubble's `.response-body` from raw text (used after streaming completes).
      10| - Sessions are snapshotted/restored by saving `chat.innerHTML` per session (`s.html`), so rendering is purely HTML-string based.
      11| - `src/panels/ChatPanelAdapter.ts` — only an adapter (`WebviewViewAdapter`) wrapping `WebviewView`/`WebviewPanel` as `PanelLike`; contains no rendering logic.
      12| - Files involved: `src/webview/WebviewRenderer.ts`, `src/webview/WebviewRenderer.test.js`, `src/panels/ChatPanelAdapter.ts`.

FILE: _audit_4.md
LINE COUNT: 20
SIZE: 1307 bytes
==========================================================================================
       1| # _audit_4 — Provider notes (src/providers)
       2| 
       3| Files under `src/providers/`: `ProviderRegistry.ts` (the only file).
       4| 
       5| (a) Provider "interface": no Provider class exists. `ProviderRegistry.ts` defines
       6| `ProviderKind = 'ollama' | 'openai' | 'copilot'` and `interface ProviderInfo { id: ProviderKind; label: string }`,
       7| mapping model-ID prefixes (`copilot::`, `openai::`, bare = ollama) to a kind via `getProviderKind()`.
       8| The real tool Provider contract is `IToolProvider` (`src/tools/providers/IToolProvider.ts`):
       9| `tools: ReadonlySet<string>` + `execute(name, args, ctx) => Promise<string>`.
      10| 
      11| (b) Registered provider names (all in `src/providers/ProviderRegistry.ts`):
      12| - `copilot` → "Copilot" (model IDs `copilot::<id>`, legacy `copilot/<id>` auto-normalized)
      13| - `openai` → "OpenAI Compatible" (model IDs `openai::<id>`)
      14| - `ollama` → "Ollama" (model IDs without prefix)
      15| No per-provider files; names live in the `KIND_LABEL` record.
      16| 
      17| (c) Tool registration: separate from src/providers/. `ToolExecutor.ts` (src/tools/ToolExecutor.ts)
      18| instantiates each `IToolProvider` (Git/Jenkins/Search/Process/Vscode/FileSystem/Atlassian/Network/
      19| Integration/DevTools/Lsp/Background/ComputerUse) in its constructor, then populates
      20| `_providerMap` (tool name → provider) from each provider's `tools` set for O(1) dispatch.

     138| +// ── Effect-based rule DSL ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
     139| +
     140| +export type ToolEffect = 'write' | 'shell' | 'network' | 'delete' | 'any';
     141| +
     142| +/**
     143| + * Single ACL rule. Stored in `amiAiClaw.toolPolicyRules` (VS Code setting).
     144| + * Rules are evaluated top-to-bottom; first match wins.
     145| + * Effects: write=file mutations, shell=commands, network=HTTP/browser, delete=destructive.
     146| + * Pattern: glob for write/delete (file path); substring for shell (command) / network (URL).
     147| + */
     148| +export interface PolicyRule {
     149| +  effect: ToolEffect;
     150| +  action: 'allow' | 'deny' | 'ask';
     151| +  pattern?: string;
     152| +}
     153| +
     154| +const _WRITE_EFFECT = new Set([
     155| +  'write_file','replace_in_file','insert_in_file','replace_all_in_file',
     156| +  'batch_replace','rename_file','copy_file','todo_write','memory_write','lsp_rename_symbol',
     157| +]);
     158| +const _SHELL_EFFECT  = new Set(['run_command','run_terminal','run_python']);
     159| +const _NET_EFFECT    = new Set(['http_request','fetch_url','browser_navigate','browser_screenshot','browser_script']);
     160| +
     161| +function _getEffect(toolName: string): ToolEffect | null {
     162| +  if (_WRITE_EFFECT.has(toolName)) return 'write';
     163| +  if (_SHELL_EFFECT.has(toolName)) return 'shell';
     164| +  if (_NET_EFFECT.has(toolName))   return 'network';
     165| +  if (toolName === 'delete_file')  return 'delete';
     166| +  return null;
     167| +}
     168| +
     169| +/** Minimal glob: * = any non-separator chars, ** = anything including /. */
     170| +function _globMatch(pattern: string, value: string): boolean {
     171| +  const v = value.replace(/\\/g, '/');
     172| +  const re = pattern
     173| +    .replace(/\\/g, '/')
     174| +    .replace(/[.+^${}()|[\]]/g, '\\$&')
     175| +    .replace(/\*\*/g, '\x00')
     176| +    .replace(/\*/g, '[^/]*')
     177| +    .replace(/\x00/g, '.*');
     178| +  // match against full path or path suffix (allows `src/**` to match `C:/ws/src/foo.ts`)
     179| +  return new RegExp(`(^|/)${re}$`, 'i').test(v) || new RegExp(`^${re}$`, 'i').test(v);
     180| +}
     181| +
     182| +/** Evaluate ordered rules; returns first matching action or null. */
     183| +export function evaluatePolicyRules(
     184| +  rules: PolicyRule[],
     185| +  effect: ToolEffect,
     186| +  value: string,
     187| +): 'allow' | 'deny' | 'ask' | null {
     188| +  for (const rule of rules) {
     189| +    if (rule.effect !== effect && rule.effect !== 'any') continue;
     190| +    if (!rule.pattern) return rule.action;
     191| +    if (effect === 'write' || effect === 'delete') {
     192| +      if (_globMatch(rule.pattern, value)) return rule.action;
     193| +    } else {
     194| +      if (value.toLowerCase().includes(rule.pattern.toLowerCase())) return rule.action;
     195| +    }
     196| +  }
     197| +  return null;
     198| +}
     199| +
     200|  /**
     201|   * 工具權限政策：alwaysAllow 集合 + 一次性確認佇列（pending promise）。

     374| +// ── TF-IDF tool search ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────
     375| +
     376| +function _tokenize(text: string): string[] {
     377| +  return text.toLowerCase().replace(/[^\w\s]/g, ' ').split(/\s+/).filter(t => t.length > 1);
     378| +}
     379| +
     380| +/** Smoothed IDF: log((N+1)/(df+1)) + 1, computed once at module load. */
     381| +const _idf: ReadonlyMap<string, number> = (() => {
     382| +  const N = EXTRA_TOOLS.length;
     383| +  const df = new Map<string, number>();
     384| +  for (const tool of EXTRA_TOOLS) {
     385| +    const terms = new Set(_tokenize(`${tool.function.name} ${tool.function.description}`));
     386| +    for (const t of terms) df.set(t, (df.get(t) ?? 0) + 1);
     387| +  }
     388| +  const idf = new Map<string, number>();
     389| +  for (const [term, count] of df) idf.set(term, Math.log((N + 1) / (count + 1)) + 1);
     390| +  return idf;
     391| +})();
     392| +
     393| +function _tfidfVec(tokens: string[]): Map<string, number> {
     394| +  const tf = new Map<string, number>();
     395| +  for (const t of tokens) tf.set(t, (tf.get(t) ?? 0) + 1);
     396| +  const vec = new Map<string, number>();
     397| +  const len = tokens.length || 1;
     398| +  // unseen terms get IDF = log((N+1)/1)+1 ≈ high weight for novel query terms
     399| +  const unseenIdf = Math.log(EXTRA_TOOLS.length + 2) + 1;
     400| +  for (const [t, count] of tf) vec.set(t, (count / len) * (_idf.get(t) ?? unseenIdf));
     401| +  return vec;
     402| +}
     403| +
     404| +function _cosine(a: Map<string, number>, b: Map<string, number>): number {
     405| +  let dot = 0, normA = 0, normB = 0;
     406| +  for (const [t, v] of a) { dot += v * (b.get(t) ?? 0); normA += v * v; }
     407| +  for (const v of b.values()) normB += v * v;
     408| +  return normA === 0 || normB === 0 ? 0 : dot / (Math.sqrt(normA) * Math.sqrt(normB));
     409| +}
     410| +
     411| +/** Pre-computed TF-IDF vectors for all EXTRA_TOOLS (avoids recompute per query). */
     412| +const _toolVecs: ReadonlyMap<string, Map<string, number>> = (() => {
     413| +  const m = new Map<string, Map<string, number>>();
     414| +  for (const tool of EXTRA_TOOLS) {
     415| +    m.set(tool.function.name, _tfidfVec(_tokenize(`${tool.function.name} ${tool.function.description}`)));
     416| +  }
     417| +  return m;
     418| +})();

     445| +  }));

       1| @echo off
       2| SETLOCAL ENABLEDELAYEDEXPANSION
       3| REM build.bat - build Ollama extension (supports local and docker builds)
       4| REM Usage: build.bat [docker|local|clean]
       5| 
       6| SET "ROOT=%~dp0"
       7| PUSHD "%ROOT%"
       8| 
       9| REM normalize host root (remove trailing backslash) for Docker volume mounts and cp
      10| set "HOSTROOT=%ROOT%"
      11| IF "%HOSTROOT:~-1%"=="\" set "HOSTROOT=%HOSTROOT:~0,-1%"
      12| 
      13| IF /I "%1"=="" (
      14|   echo No argument specified - auto-selecting build method...
      15|   REM prefer local if npm available, else use docker
      16|   npm --version >nul 2>&1
      17|   IF NOT ERRORLEVEL 1 GOTO local
      18|   docker info >nul 2>&1
      19|   IF NOT ERRORLEVEL 1 GOTO docker
      20|   docker --version >nul 2>&1
      21|   IF NOT ERRORLEVEL 1 (
      22|     echo Docker CLI is installed, but the Docker engine is not running.
      23|     echo Start Docker Desktop or use: build.bat local
      24|     GOTO fail
      25|   )
      26|   echo Neither npm nor Docker was found. Please install Node.js or Docker.
      27|   GOTO fail
      28| )
      29| 
      30| IF /I "%1"=="local" GOTO local
      31| IF /I "%1"=="docker" GOTO docker
      32| IF /I "%1"=="clean" GOTO clean
      33| IF /I "%1"=="ollama" GOTO ollama_cmd
      34| IF /I "%1"=="help" GOTO usage
      35| 
      36| :usage
      37| echo Usage: build.bat [docker^|local^|clean^|ollama]
      38| echo.
      39| echo   (no args) - auto-select: local npm if available, else Docker
      40| echo   local      - build locally (npm ci, compile, package)
      41| echo   docker     - build inside Docker and extract ./dist
      42| echo   clean      - remove node_modules, out, dist
      43| echo   ollama     - manage Ollama Docker service:
      44| echo     start    - start Ollama server  (docker compose up -d ollama)
      45| echo     stop     - stop  Ollama server  (docker compose down)
      46| echo     pull     - pull a model, e.g.: build.bat ollama pull llama3
      47| POPD
      48| ENDLOCAL
      49| EXIT /B 0
      50| 
      51| :local
      52| echo ---------- Local build (npm via Docker if available) ----------
      53| if not exist package.json (
      54|   echo package.json not found. Are you in the extension folder?
      55|   GOTO fail
      56| )
      57| 
      58| REM compute host path without trailing backslash for Docker -v
      59| set "HOSTROOT=%ROOT%"
      60| IF "%HOSTROOT:~-1%"=="\" set "HOSTROOT=%HOSTROOT:~0,-1%"
      61| 
      62| docker --version >nul 2>&1
      63| IF NOT ERRORLEVEL 1 (
      64|   echo Using Docker to run npm commands inside node:20-slim
      65|   echo Installing dependencies - npm install...
      66|   call docker run --rm -v "%HOSTROOT%:/workspace" -w /workspace node:20-slim sh -c "apt-get update -qq && apt-get install -y --no-install-recommends git > /dev/null 2>&1 && npm install --no-audit --no-fund"
      67|   IF ERRORLEVEL 1 GOTO fail
      68|   echo Compiling TypeScript...
      69|   call docker run --rm -v "%HOSTROOT%:/workspace" -w /workspace node:20-slim sh -c "npm run compile"
      70|   IF ERRORLEVEL 1 GOTO fail
      71|   echo Packaging VSIX...
      72|   call docker run --rm -v "%HOSTROOT%:/workspace" -w /workspace node:20-slim sh -c "npm run package"
      73|   IF ERRORLEVEL 1 GOTO fail
      74|   echo Local build ^(via Docker^) finished. Output: %ROOT%dist
      75|   GOTO done
      76| ) ELSE (
      77|   echo Docker not found, falling back to local npm
      78|   IF NOT EXIST node_modules (
      79|     echo Installing dependencies - npm ci...
      80|     call npm ci
      81|     IF ERRORLEVEL 1 GOTO fail
      82|   ) ELSE (
      83|     echo Using existing node_modules
      84|   )
      85|   echo Compiling TypeScript...
      86|   call npm run compile
      87|   IF ERRORLEVEL 1 GOTO fail
      88|   echo Packaging VSIX...
      89|   call npm run package
      90|   IF ERRORLEVEL 1 GOTO fail
      91|   echo Local build finished. Output: %ROOT%dist
      92|   GOTO done
      93| )
      94| 
      95| :docker
      96| echo ---------- Docker build ----------
      97| docker --version >nul 2>&1
      98| IF ERRORLEVEL 1 (
      99|   echo Docker not found. Install Docker or use "local" build.
     100|   GOTO fail
     101| )
     102| docker info >nul 2>&1
     103| IF ERRORLEVEL 1 (
     104|   echo Docker CLI is installed, but the Docker engine is not running.
     105|   echo Start Docker Desktop, then rerun build.bat docker.
     106|   GOTO fail
     107| )
     108| if exist "%HOSTROOT%\dist" rd /s /q "%HOSTROOT%\dist"
     109| echo Building docker image and extracting dist...
     110| call docker build -f "%HOSTROOT%\Dockerfile" -t ollama-chat-builder --output "type=local,dest=%HOSTROOT%\dist" "%HOSTROOT%"
     111| IF ERRORLEVEL 1 GOTO fail
     112| if exist "%HOSTROOT%\dist\amiclaw.vsix" (
     113|   echo Docker build succeeded. Artifact: %HOSTROOT%\dist\amiclaw.vsix
     114|   GOTO done
     115| ) else (
     116|   echo Build finished but vsix not found in dist\.
     117|   dir "%HOSTROOT%\dist" 2>nul
     118|   GOTO fail
     119| )
     120| 
     121| :ollama_cmd
     122| docker --version >nul 2>&1
     123| IF ERRORLEVEL 1 (
     124|   echo Docker is required for the ollama command.
     125|   GOTO fail
     126| )
     127| IF /I "%2"=="start" (
     128|   echo Starting Ollama server via docker compose...
     129|   call docker compose up -d ollama
     130|   IF ERRORLEVEL 1 GOTO fail
     131|   echo Ollama is running at http://localhost:11434
     132|   echo Pull a model: build.bat ollama pull llama3
     133|   GOTO done
     134| )
     135| IF /I "%2"=="stop" (
     136|   echo Stopping Ollama server...
     137|   call docker compose down
     138|   IF ERRORLEVEL 1 GOTO fail
     139|   GOTO done
     140| )
     141| IF /I "%2"=="pull" (
     142|   IF "%3"=="" (
     143|     echo Usage: build.bat ollama pull ^<model-name^>
     144|     echo Example: build.bat ollama pull llama3
     145|     GOTO fail
     146|   )
     147|   echo Pulling model %3 from Ollama server...
     148|   call docker compose exec ollama ollama pull %3
     149|   IF ERRORLEVEL 1 GOTO fail
     150|   GOTO done
     151| )
     152| echo Unknown ollama sub-command: %2
     153| echo Valid: start, stop, pull
     154| GOTO fail
     155| 
     156| :clean
     157| echo Cleaning directories...
     158| if exist node_modules rd /s /q node_modules
     159| if exist out rd /s /q out
     160| if exist dist rd /s /q dist
     161| echo Clean complete.
     162| GOTO done
     163| 
     164| :fail
     165| echo ERROR: build failed.
     166| POPD
     167| ENDLOCAL
     168| EXIT /B 1
     169| 
     170| :done
     171| echo Done.
     172| POPD
     173| ENDLOCAL
     174| EXIT /B 0
